The instruction says use 3-4-4,\n        but 10-digit numbers cannot be 3-4-4. We do 3-3-4 fallback for 10 digits.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    d = _digits_only(raw)\n    if len(d) == 11:\n        return f\"{d[0:3]}-{d[3:7]}-{d[7:11]}\"\n    if len(d) == 10:\n        return f\"{d[0:3]}-{d[3:6]}-{d[6:10]}\"\n    # Best-effort: don't invent digits\n    return raw\n\n\ndef parse_yyyy_mm_dd(s: str) -> date:\n    y, m, d = s.split(\"-\")\n    return date(int(y), int(m), int(d))\n\n\ndef korean_full_year_age(birth: date, visit: date) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Full-year age (만 나이): subtract 1 if birthday not yet reached by visit date.\"\"\"\n\n    age = visit.year - birth.year\n    if (visit.month, visit.day) < (birth.month, birth.day):\n        age -= 1\n    return age\n\n\ndef localname(tag: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Return localname for ET tag in {ns}name form.\"\"\"\n\n    if tag.startswith(\"{\"):\n        return tag.split(\"}\", 1)[1]\n    return tag\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReplacementSummary:\n    file_path: str\n    paragraphs_changed: int\n    placeholders_replaced: int\n\n\ndef build_replacements(patient: Dict[str, str]) -> Tuple[Dict[str, str], Dict[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Return (raw_map, placeholder_map) where placeholder_map is keyed by '{{key}}'.\"\"\"\n\n    raw = dict(patient)\n\n    # Phone normalization\n    if \"회신전화\" in raw:\n        raw[\"회신전화\"] = normalize_phone_korean_mobile(str(raw[\"회신전화\"]))\n\n    # Age annotation appended to birth date\n    if \"생년월일\" in raw and \"방문일\" in raw:\n        b = parse_yyyy_mm_dd(str(raw[\"생년월일\"]))\n        v = parse_yyyy_mm_dd(str(raw[\"방문일\"]))\n        age = korean_full_year_age(b, v)\n        raw[\"생년월일\"] = f\"{raw['생년월일']} ({age}세)\"\n\n    placeholders = {f\"{{{{{k}}}}}\": str(v) for k, v in raw.items()}\n    return raw, placeholders\n\n\ndef replace_in_paragraph(p: ET.Element, placeholder_map: Dict[str, str]) -> Tuple[bool, int]:\n    \"\"\"Replace placeholders inside a paragraph.\n\n    Returns (changed, replaced_count).\n\n    Strategy:\n    - Collect all descendant *text nodes* from <hp:t> elements in document order.\n    - Concatenate them, do placeholder replacement on the combined string.\n    - If changed, write the whole replaced string back into the first <hp:t> and clear the rest.\n\n    This is robust to placeholders split across multiple <hp:t> elements.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # HWPX text elements are typically hp:t; we look for any element whose localname is 't'.\n    t_nodes: List[ET.Element] = [\n        el for el in p.iter() if localname(el.tag) == \"t\"\n    ]\n\n    if not t_nodes:\n        return False, 0\n\n    original_parts = [(el.text or \"\") for el in t_nodes]\n    original = \"\".join(original_parts)\n\n    if \"{{\" not in original:\n        return False, 0\n\n    replaced = original\n    replaced_count = 0\n    for ph, val in placeholder_map.items():\n        if ph in replaced:\n            # Count occurrences before replacement for summary\n            replaced_count += replaced.count(ph)\n            replaced = replaced.replace(ph, val)\n\n    changed = replaced != original\n    if not changed:\n        return False, 0\n\n    # Write back: keep element structure, but consolidate text into the first node.\n    t_nodes[0].text = replaced\n    for el in t_nodes[1:]:\n        el.text = \"\"\n\n    return True, replaced_count\n\n\ndef remove_layout_caches_from_paragraph(p: ET.Element) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Remove line-seg/layout cache elements from a paragraph.\n\n    In HWPX these often appear as hp:linesegarray (as observed in the template).\n    We remove any direct child whose localname contains 'lineseg' (case-insensitive).\n\n    Returns number of removed children.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    removed = 0\n    for child in list(p):\n        ln = localname(child.tag).lower()\n        if \"lineseg\" in ln:\n            p.remove(child)\n            removed += 1\n    return removed\n\n\ndef process_xml_file(path: Path, placeholder_map: Dict[str, str]) -> Optional[ReplacementSummary]:\n    \"\"\"Process one XML file; returns summary if any placeholder replacements were attempted.\"\"\"\n\n    try:\n        tree = ET.parse(path)\n    except ET.ParseError as e:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"XML parse error in {path}: {e}\")\n\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    paragraphs_changed = 0\n    placeholders_replaced = 0\n\n    # Paragraphs are typically hp:p; we match by localname 'p'\n    for p in root.iter():\n        if localname(p.tag) != \"p\":\n            continue\n\n        changed, replaced_count = replace_in_paragraph(p, placeholder_map)\n        if changed:\n            paragraphs_changed += 1\n            placeholders_replaced += replaced_count\n            remove_layout_caches_from_paragraph(p)\n\n    if placeholders_replaced == 0 and paragraphs_changed == 0:\n        #
